# ACT PATH Golden Reference

> Generated by `golden_act_path.c`  
> Functional / step-level reference for padding, patch order, packing, FC mapping, and skew distribution.

Coverage: Conv Cin=3/6 x padding=0/1, generic FC, FC0 packing sample, and all 4096 FC0 values.

PASS checks the C model; RTL clock timing, memory latency, and stalls are not simulated.

## Data Path

```text
RAM -> act_ld_unit -> input_buf -> act_patch_gen / fc_gen -> MUX -> act_feeder -> act_skew -> PE
```

## Patch Movement Example

For `padding = 1`, the 4×4 window moves by **2 pixels**.

```text
Padded row : 0  A  B  C  D  E  F ...

Window 0   : [0  A  B  C]
Patch 1    : [0  A  B]
Patch 2    :    [A  B  C]

Window 1   :       [B  C  D  E]
Patch 1    :       [B  C  D]
Patch 2    :          [C  D  E]
```

## CONV Path

```text
Config : input=4x4x3, kernel=3x3, stride=1, pad=1, K=27
Packing: byte0/1/2 = C0/C1/C2; Cin=6 adds a second word for C3/C4/C5.
Patch order inside each 2x2 block: TL -> TR -> BL -> BR
Input values start from 1 so real data is not confused with padding 0.

```

### First 4×4 Window

```text
Out-of-range pixels use zero in every channel group.

  Channel 0
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |   0 |   0 |   0 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |   1 |   2 |   3 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |   5 |   6 |   7 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |   9 |  10 |  11 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+

  Channel 1
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |   0 |   0 |   0 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |  21 |  22 |  23 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |  25 |  26 |  27 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |  29 |  30 |  31 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+

  Channel 2
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |   0 |   0 |   0 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |  41 |  42 |  43 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |  45 |  46 |  47 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+
  |   0 |  49 |  50 |  51 |
  +-----+-----+-----+-----+

```

### Patch / Feeder / Skew Trace

```text
FEED rows = {Row1,Row2,Row3}; packed word = {Row3,Row2,Row1}
SKEW      = {Row1(now), Row2(-1), Row3(-2)}

```

## CONV Path

```text
Config : input=4x4x6, kernel=3x3, stride=1, pad=1, K=54
Packing: byte0/1/2 = C0/C1/C2; Cin=6 adds a second word for C3/C4/C5.
Patch order inside each 2x2 block: TL -> TR -> BL -> BR
Input values start from 1 so real data is not confused with padding 0.

```
